*Pui Chung Siu,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间异质生物医学知识图谱（THBKG），它记录了目标-疾病连接证据随时间变化的信息，允许按历史日期重建证据画像，并定义了一个与临床决策对齐的基准来预测靶向-疾病组合能否从II期推进到III期。图传播方法在该基准上显著优于直接证据模型，尤其在72.8%无直接证据的样本上表现突出，并支持可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 靶向-疾病之间的错误关联是II期临床疗效失败的40-50%原因，因此需要一种能在药物进入临床时评估其靶向-疾病关联证据质量的方法。现有生物医学知识图谱无法按过去时间点重建证据画像，这阻碍了回顾性验证和新药研发决策。

Method: 构建THBKG时间异质生物医学知识图谱（110,396个实体、1110万条边、19种关系类型，每条边记录证据变化的年份），定义决策对齐基准（decision-aligned benchmark）预测II期到III期的推进，并使用图传播（graph propagation）方法在决策时间点前的子图上进行预测，同时用路径可解释器对预测进行分解。

Result: 图传播方法在决策对齐协议下优于所有直接证据参考模型，在前十靶向-疾病对中相对成功率达到4.3-4.5。在72.8%无直接靶向-疾病证据的样本上，编码器排名仍比随机高出5-6倍，通过中间生物通路恢复信号。

Conclusion: THBKG为生物医学靶向假设的回顾性验证提供了持续更新的基础资源，能够按历史时点重建证据画像并支持可解释的决策预测，有助于提高新药研发中靶向-疾病关联判断的准确性。

Abstract: Inadequate target--disease linkage accounts for 40--50\% of Phase~II efficacy failures, so anticipating which programmes will advance would let sponsors back the hypotheses most likely to reach patients. What a programme can be judged on is the evidence that supported its linkage \emph{when it entered the clinic}. No existing biomedical knowledge graph allows that evidence profile to be assembled as of a past date. We present the Temporal Heterogeneous Biomedical Knowledge Graph (THBKG), which describes and predicts therapeutic target--disease links through time: 110,396 entities and 11.1M edges across nineteen relation types, each edge carrying the year its evidence changed, so a pair's profile can be recovered as it stood when its own decision fell due. On this graph we define a decision-aligned benchmark that predicts, for a target--disease pair entering Phase~II, whether it advances to Phase~III on evidence datable before that decision. Graph propagation over the THBKG outranks every direct-evidence reference scored under the same decision-aligned protocol, reaching a relative success of 4.3--4.5 at the top ten pairs per therapeutic area. The gain concentrates on the 72.8\% of pairs with no direct target--disease evidence at their decision point, where a direct-edge model has nothing to read: the encoders still rank five- to sixfold above chance, recovering the signal by propagating over the intervening biology. Adapting a path-based explainer to the decision-time subgraph decomposes each prediction into the evidence landscape behind the hypothesis for explainable prediction. We release the THBKG as a continually updated substrate for studying therapeutic target hypotheses by retrospective validation.

</details>


### [48] [Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Reinforcement Learning for Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05989)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OG-SPR，一种用于连续控制的无模型视觉RL算法，通过结合多步潜在空间自预测与下一观测预测两种辅助目标，学习既具有时序可预测性又扎根于观测层动态的表征。实验在28个DeepMind Control Suite任务上超越现有最先进方法，尤其在dog和humanoid等困难任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动态的表征学习方法分为两类：潜在空间自预测和观测空间预测，但这两类方法在训练数据有限的情况下仍难以应对具有挑战性的视觉控制任务。仅依赖单一预测目标可能不足以学习到高质量的表征：观测预测将表征扎根于观测层动态，但缺乏对潜在表征在长时间跨度上的时序可预测性的直接约束。

Method: OG-SPR包含两个核心辅助目标：多步潜在自预测和下一观测预测。为克服直接在共享表征上施加潜在自预测可能过度约束表征并损害性能的问题，OG-SPR引入了两个轻量级适配器（adapter）用于潜在自预测，使共享表征能够从时序预测信号中获益，而无需被迫直接满足自预测目标。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite的28个视觉控制任务上进行实验，OG-SPR在整体性能上优于最先进的自预测和观测预测RL方法，尤其在dog和humanoid等具有挑战性的任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 实证表明仅依赖单一预测目标（自预测或观测预测）可能不足够；通过自适应器解耦潜在自预测与共享表征，可以同时利用两种预测目标的优势，从而在数据有限的情况下学习到更优的视觉表征，提升无模型视觉RL的样本效率。

Abstract: Sample-efficient policy learning from pixels is a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Recent dynamics-based representation learning methods have significantly improved the sample efficiency of model-free visual RL by learning dynamics-aware representations through auxiliary prediction performed either in latent space (self-prediction) or observation space (observation prediction). However, state-of-the-art methods from both categories still struggle on challenging visual control tasks when training data is limited. We posit that relying on either predictive objective alone may be insufficient. In contrast, observation prediction grounds learned representations in observation-level dynamics, but does not directly regularize the temporal predictability of latent representations over extended horizons. In this paper, we propose Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Representations (OG-SPR), a model-free visual RL algorithm for continuous control that learns representations that are both temporally predictive in latent space and grounded in observation-level dynamics. OG-SPR incorporates two core auxiliary objectives: multi-step latent self-prediction and next-observation prediction. We empirically show that directly imposing latent self-prediction on the shared representation may over-constrain it and does not necessarily improve performance. To address this issue, OG-SPR introduces two lightweight adapters for latent self-prediction, allowing the shared representation to benefit from temporally predictive signals without being forced to directly satisfy the self-prediction objective. Experiments on 28 visual control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite show that OG-SPR improves aggregate performance over state-of-the-art self-predictive and observation-predictive RL methods, with particularly pronounced gains in challenging domains such as dog and humanoid.

</details>


### [49] [A Unified Risk View of Uncertainty: Posterior Risk for Disentanglement and Evaluation Beyond Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05995)
*Frieder Wizgall,Georg Tirpitz,Moritz Seiler,Kerstin Ritter,Bálint Mucsányi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将不确定性统一定义为逐点后验风险（期望损失），并据此构建一个基于半合成数据集的基准，能够直接计算oracle认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，从而避免使用代理任务（如OOD检测）进行不确定性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用中可靠的不确定性估计至关重要，但现有文献对认知不确定性和偶然不确定性的定义不一致，且缺乏真实的不确定性ground-truth，使得评估方法的质量变得困难。现有基准主要依赖OOD检测等代理任务，无法提供完整的地面真值不确定性目标，也无法深入分析不确定性估计的结构和质量。

Method: 作者提出将不确定性统一定义为逐点后验风险，即预测器在给定数据下关于合理ground-truth函数分布的期望损失。该视角将贝叶斯函数不确定性（认知不确定性）与估计器依赖的后验均值偏差（如模型设定错误和优化误差）相结合。基于这一定义，利用真实协变量和已知生成过程的半合成数据集，构建能够直接计算oracle认知与偶然不确定性的理论驱动基准。

Result: 实验发现，准确的预测并不能保证可靠的不确定性解耦。该基准揭示了方法之间实际有用的差异，识别出与oracle不确定性目标具有有意义对齐的方法，同时也暴露了对数据集和建模选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过统一的不确定性定义和直接计算oracle不确定性的基准，该工作为不确定性估计提供了更精确的评估框架，避免了代理任务的局限性，能够对不确定性估计进行细粒度分析。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are critical in safety-sensitive applications, where understanding the sources of predictive uncertainty is essential. This often requires disentangling epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty, yet these uncertainty types are not defined consistently across the literature, making it difficult to assess whether a method produces accurate uncertainty estimates. Evaluation is further complicated by the fact that ground-truth epistemic uncertainty is typically unavailable. Existing benchmarks therefore mostly rely on proxy tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not provide complete ground-truth uncertainty targets and offer limited insight into the structure and quality of uncertainty estimates. We propose a unified definition of uncertainty as pointwise posterior risk, the expected loss of a predictor under the distribution of plausible ground-truth functions given the data. This view combines Bayesian uncertainty over functions with estimator-dependent deviations from the posterior mean, capturing effects such as misspecification and optimization error. This formulation constitutes the foundation of a theory-backed benchmark that enables direct computation of oracle epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty using semi-synthetic datasets with real covariates and known generative processes. By avoiding proxy evaluations, the benchmark enables fine-grained analysis of uncertainty estimates. Empirically, we find that accurate prediction does not guarantee reliable uncertainty disentanglement. The benchmark reveals practically useful differences between methods, identifying approaches with meaningful alignment to oracle uncertainty targets while exposing sensitivity to datasets and modeling choices.

</details>


### [50] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Agree with Themselves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06004)
*Christian Klötergens,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Tom Hanika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种一致性检验方法（边缘化一致性和分解一致性）来评估表格基础模型（TFM）自回归生成多元预测时所产生联合分布的忠实性。实验发现所有被评估的TFM在分类和回归任务的所有数据集上均违反这两种一致性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）是目前表格预测问题的最佳方法，通过自回归方式将单变量预测器转换为多元预测器。然而，这种转换产生的联合分布是否忠实于真实数据分布从未被研究过。由于真实后验分布未知，无法在真实数据集上直接评估TFM，因此需要一个不依赖地面真实分布的一致性框架来检验模型预测是否能从任何联合分布中产生。

Method: 本文提出两个一致性条件：(1) 边缘化一致性——要求模型直接预测的边缘分布与其通过边缘化联合条件分布得到的边缘分布相等；(2) 分解一致性——要求不同的因子分解顺序（不同的自回归采样顺序）产生相等的联合分布。通过对多种TFM在分类和回归任务上进行系统评估来检验这两个条件。

Result: 所有被评估的TFM在分类和回归任务的所有数据集上都违反了边缘化一致性和分解一致性这两个要求，表明这些模型自回归生成的多元预测联合分布不一致且依赖于采样顺序。

Conclusion: TFM自回归生成的多元联合分布在结构上存在不一致性问题，其预测结果不能被认为是来自任何一致的联合分布。这提示研究者需要重新审视TFM在多变量预测场景中的可靠性，并可能需要开发新的方法以确保联合分布的一致性。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are currently the best approach to tabular prediction problems. They are constructed as transformers that approximate the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution based on a pre-training prior. These univariate predictors can be converted into multivariate ones autoregressively by sampling one target and adding it to the features.
  However, the faithfulness of the resulting joint has not been investigated. Furthermore, TFMs cannot be evaluated against the posterior itself, at least not on real-world datasets, because the ground-truth distribution is unknown. We therefore propose asking a different question: could a model's predictions result from any joint distribution? To answer this question, we pose two requirements that any such model must satisfy. The first is marginalization consistency, which demands that marginalized conditionals are equal to directly predicted marginals. The second is factorization consistency, which demands that different factorization orders result in equal joint distributions. Every TFM that we evaluate violates both of these requirements for both classification and regression across all datasets.

</details>


### [51] [ProDVI: Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06015)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProDVI提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）生成可执行的Python程序来编码环境动力学先验知识，从而初始化价值网络的框架。该方法通过生成的合成转移数据预训练actor-critic结构中的状态-动作编码器，为在线RL提供动力学感知的归纳偏置，无需预收集数据集、高保真模拟器或元学习等外部资源，在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control Suite上显著提升了无模型RL算法的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习（RL）样本效率极低，主要原因是智能体从零开始初始化，需要在线交互逐步获取任务相关知识。现有方法依赖预收集数据集、高保真模拟器或多任务元学习等先备条件，但这些资源往往难以获取甚至不可用。因此需要在无需这些外部资源的前提下，利用更易获得的常识和领域知识来为RL智能体提供有效的初始化。

Method: ProDVI框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 提示代码生成式大语言模型产生编码环境动力学粗略假设的可执行Python函数；(2) 利用这些函数生成合成转移数据（synthetic transitions）；(3) 基于合成数据构建辅助动力学预测目标，对actor-critic框架中价值网络的状态-动作编码器进行预训练。预训练得到的表示提供动力学感知的归纳偏置，随后在线RL阶段可通过真实转移数据和奖励予以纠正。关键设计是生成程序仅用于表示预训练，不要求精确模拟目标环境。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control Suite的标准任务上进行了实验验证，结果表明ProDVI能够有效提升无模型RL算法的样本效率。即使生成的动力学程序不够精确，其诱导的初始化通过在线学习后依然能够被有效修正，体现出框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ProDVI证明了将LLM编码的常识和领域知识转化为可执行程序并用其预训练价值网络表示的可行性，为RL智能体初始化提供了一种不依赖昂贵外部资源的新范式，有效改善无模型RL的样本效率。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) is notoriously sample inefficient. One contributing factor is that RL agents are typically initialized from scratch, forcing them to acquire task-relevant knowledge through online interaction. Existing approaches obtain informative initializations through pre-collected datasets, high-fidelity simulators, or meta-learning over related tasks, but these prerequisites may be difficult to access or even unavailable. In this paper, we propose Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization (ProDVI), a framework that leverages the commonsense and domain knowledge encoded in large language models to initialize RL agents without relying on these resources. Specifically, ProDVI prompts a code-generating language model to produce executable Python functions that encode coarse hypotheses about environment dynamics. These functions are then used to generate synthetic transitions. Based on these transitions, we construct an auxiliary dynamics prediction objective to pretrain the state-action encoder of the value network in an actor-critic framework. The learned representation provides dynamics-aware inductive biases before online RL begins. Importantly, the generated programs are used only for representation pretraining and are not required to faithfully simulate the target environment. While the generated programs may be inaccurate, their induced initialization can be corrected through online learning from real transitions and rewards. Experiments on OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that ProDVI can effectively improve the sample efficiency of model-free RL algorithms.

</details>


### [52] [BioKD: Selective Physiology-to-Video Knowledge Distillation via Reliability Gate for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06023)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Hongjun Liu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioKD，一种可靠性感知的生理信号到视频知识蒸馏框架，用于视频情感识别。训练时利用生理信号作为特权信息引导视频学生模型，推理时仅依赖非侵入式视频输入。通过样本级可靠性门控机制和渐进式蒸馏策略抑制噪声生理监督的负迁移，在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上取得优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频情感识别在行为线索模糊或被社会性掩饰时表现受限，而生理信号虽可靠但部署性差。现有跨模态蒸馏方法未充分考虑生理教师信号本身的高噪声、不稳定性和受试者间差异，导致负迁移。

Method: BioKD框架包含三个核心组件：(1)以生理信号作为特权信息训练视频学生模型；(2)样本级可靠性感知门控机制，动态评估生理教师监督的可靠性；(3)渐进式蒸馏策略，自适应调节知识迁移强度，抑制不可靠监督带来的负迁移。

Result: 在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上，BioKD在trial-wise和subject-wise两种评估协议下均优于代表性基线。DEAP trial-wise唤醒度达到68.01%，subject-wise设置下达到65.29%。消融分析显示可靠性建模优于熵值加权策略，且推理阶段零额外开销。

Conclusion: BioKD有效解决了生理教师信号不可靠性导致的负迁移问题，验证了显式建模监督可靠性对跨模态知识蒸馏的重要性，同时消除了推理时对生理传感和多模态同步的需求，具备良好的实用部署价值。

Abstract: To address the limitations of video-based emotion recognition under ambiguous or socially masked behavioral cues, as well as the poor deployability of physiological signals, this paper proposes a reliability-aware physiology-to-video knowledge distillation framework, termed BioKD. The proposed framework leverages physiological signals as privileged information during training to guide a video-based student model in learning deep affective representations, while relying solely on non-intrusive video inputs at inference time. To cope with the high noise and instability of physiological teacher supervision caused by inter-subject variability, signal artifacts, and temporal inconsistency, BioKD incorporates a sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism together with a progressive distillation strategy. By adaptively regulating the strength of knowledge transfer, the framework suppresses negative transfer induced by unreliable physiological supervision and enables more stable cross-modal distillation. Experiments on DEAP and AMIGOS show that BioKD consistently outperforms representative baselines under both trial-wise and subject-wise evaluation protocols for valence and arousal recognition. For example, BioKD achieves 68.01\% on DEAP (trial-wise arousal) and 65.29\% under the more challenging subject-wise setting, demonstrating improved performance under a subject-independent evaluation setting. Further analyses show that BioKD effectively mitigates overconfident teacher errors and outperforms an entropy-only weighting strategy, confirming the importance of explicitly modeling supervision reliability. In addition, BioKD introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture and removes the need for physiological sensing and multimodal synchronization.

</details>


### [53] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoThread，一种混合自适应线程调优方法，用于缓解强化学习推理中的仿真器瓶颈。该方法利用物理信息神经算子(PINO)作为线程数预测器，并结合有限源M/M/1排队模型来约束和引导预测，同时通过负载感知的在线微调来补偿预测误差。实验表明，AutoThread相比静态策略平均加速18.4%，吞吐量分别是XGBoost和Reinforcer的1.7倍和1.8倍，相比最先进方法执行时间最多减少83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真在环决策系统中，强化学习推理受限于仿真器侧的执行开销，工作负载高度动态且对运行时线程配置敏感。现有的多线程策略难以在执行前或执行中匹配线程资源，导致资源争用、调度开销和吞吐量下降。核心动机是通过识别关键因素（任务执行时间与调度时间的比值）来动态确定最优线程数，从而缓解仿真瓶颈。

Method: AutoThread采用三层方法：(1) 使用物理信息神经算子(PINO)作为线程数预测器，具备快速准确的预测能力；(2) 引入有限源M/M/1排队模型对预测进行约束和引导，确保预测符合系统排队特征；(3) 执行负载感知的在线微调，补偿预测误差并根据实际负载动态细化资源分配。

Result: AutoThread相比静态策略平均加速18.4%，平均吞吐量为XGBoost的1.7倍、Reinforcer的1.8倍，相比最先进方法执行时间最多减少83.8%。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 通过实证分析识别出任务执行时间与调度时间之比是决定最优线程数的关键因素，并据此设计了结合PINO预测器与排队模型的混合自适应方法，有效解决了动态工作负载下的线程资源匹配问题，显著提升了RL推理的仿真吞吐量和执行效率。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [54] [Dynamic Graph Prompting via Topology-Routed Mixed-Curvature Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06031)
*Quanxin Wang,Xuanting Xie,Bingheng Li,Xingtong Yu,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurvPrompt，一种面向动态图的拓扑路由几何提示框架。它解决了现有动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中操作而导致的"几何欠适应"问题——即局部拓扑随时间演化重组边曲率谱，最优表示几何应相应演变。CurvPrompt维护一组曲率多样的黎曼专家（每个配可学习提示），通过拓扑感知门控将每个节点-时间实例路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化的混合曲率表示。预训练用软路由建立连续拓扑-几何映射，下游适应时转为硬Top-K路由（均匀权重），在四个基准数据集上显著提升少样本链接预测并稳定提升节点分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图提示方法在单一固定嵌入空间中操作（如欧几里得空间），但研究表明动态图中局部聚类和度异质性的时间偏移会重组边曲率谱，表明最优表示几何应随局部拓扑动态演变。这种"最优几何动态变化"与"固定单一空间"之间的不匹配被正式化为几何欠适应（geometry under-adaptation）问题，此前未被解决。

Method: CurvPrompt包含三个核心组件：（1）曲率多样黎曼专家库——维护多个具有不同曲率的黎曼几何空间，每个空间配备可学习提示；（2）拓扑感知门控——根据节点-时间实例的局部拓扑特征，动态将其路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化混合曲率表示；（3）两阶段路由策略——预训练阶段使用软路由以构建连续的拓扑-几何映射保证训练稳定性，下游标签稀缺适应阶段切换为硬Top-K路由（均匀权重）以提升参数效率和泛化能力。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CurvPrompt在少样本链接预测任务上取得显著提升，并在节点分类任务上表现出强且一致的性能。实验结果验证了几何自适应提示在动态图场景中的必要性。

Conclusion: CurvPrompt通过引入混合曲率黎曼专家和拓扑感知路由机制，有效解决了动态图提示中的几何欠适应问题。其核心洞见是：动态图的表示几何应与局部拓扑的时间演化同步调整，而不是固守单一空间。实验结果证实了几何自适应提示策略的有效性，为动态图label-scarce场景下的参数高效适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamic graph prompting freezes a pre-trained temporal backbone and adapts it to label-scarce downstream tasks using lightweight prompts. However, existing methods operate within a single, fixed embedding space. In this work, we reveal that temporal shifts in local clustering and degree heterogeneity actively reorganize the edge curvature spectrum---indicating that the optimal representation geometry dynamically evolves with local topology over time. We formalize this unaddressed mismatch as geometry under-adaptation. To overcome this limitation, we propose CurvPrompt, a topology-routed geometry prompting framework for dynamic graphs. Instead of relying on a single space, CurvPrompt maintains a bank of curvature-diverse Riemannian experts, each paired with a learnable prompt. A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [55] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在9个北极航运季节的3,186条真实船舶航迹上，系统评估了线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和隐变量上下文IRL模型。结果显示非线性奖励比线性基准在留出集似然上提升50.9%，而添加船舶特定隐上下文反而使性能下降16.5%。行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验表明，表面上的船舶级行为变异主要可由可观测的航线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。此外，不同评估指标（预测精度、路线保真度、奖励迁移）产生不同的模型排名，说明单一指标不足以评估学习到的奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航可为北极航运提供应对海冰变化的能力，但可靠部署需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。IRL可从船舶轨迹中恢复此类奖励，而元IRL方法引入隐上下文变量以捕捉行为异质性。然而，这些隐表示是否真正恢复了隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了观测状态中已有的信息，这一问题尚不明确。作者旨在通过受控评估来回答这一关键问题，以支持安全关键领域的可信AI部署。

Method: 作者在9个北极航运季节、202艘船的3,186条AIS衍生航迹上进行了受控评估，比较了三种模型：线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励、以及基于相同非线性架构构建的隐上下文模型。评估方法包括留出集似然比较、行为分析、上下文探测以及预注册的特征隐藏消融实验。同时他们还从预测精度、路线保真度和奖励迁移等多个维度评估模型，以考察不同指标对模型排名的影响。

Result: 非线性奖励比线性基线在留出似然上提升50.9%；添加船舶特定隐上下文使性能降低16.5%。行为分析、上下文探测和特征隐藏消融一致表明，表面上的船舶级行为变异主要由可观测的航线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。此外，预测精度、路线保真度和奖励迁移产生了不同的模型排名，证明单一指标不足以评价学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 在添加每船舶隐上下文之前，应首先测试可观测的路线、环境和船舶特征是否已经解释了行为变异。这一发现支持更可信的AI在安全关键领域（如北极航运导航）中的部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [56] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor方法，将确定性物理基础模型适配为高效的生成式替代模型，通过两阶段推理（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨率）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配三个关键组件，在准确率、谱一致性和计算效率上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归机器学习模拟器在长时程预测中存在误差累积问题，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性，需要一种既能保持物理保真度又具备计算效率的可扩展替代方案。

Method: 1) 两阶段推理方案：结合大步长因果自回归模型与非因果时间超分辨率网络，在减少误差累积的同时降低计算成本；2) 均值预测正则化（MPR）：新的训练目标，在零噪声条件下约束生成模型预测确定性分布均值，显著提升FGN和扩散模拟器的性能与稳定性；3) 空间梯度匹配：提升模拟精度和物理保真度（通过功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多个模拟数据集上，模型在预测精度、谱一致性和计算效率上均优于对比方法。相比基于Walrus微调方法学的参考方法，平均降低42.9%的预测误差；在10个数据集中有8个在方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）上优于Walrus。

Conclusion: Kastor证明通过精心设计的推理方案、正则化目标和物理一致性约束，可以有效将确定性物理基础模型转化为高性能的生成式模拟器，在保持物理保真度的同时实现显著的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [57] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自预训练（SPT）在医学时间序列任务中对Transformer模型的性能提升效果，涵盖多模态、多变量和单变量的三种代表性医学任务，发现SPT可稳定提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，且深层模型受益更大，是一种无需任务特定架构修改的通用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期证据表明Transformer架构在长上下文化基准上受益于自预训练（SPT），但尚不清楚这种增益是否扩展到医学时间序列领域（包括多模态、多变量乃至简单单变量数据）。作者希望在数据有限条件下评估SPT对模型性能和可扩展性的影响，以探索提升医疗应用中Transformer鲁棒性的通用方法。

Method: 在三个代表性医学时间序列任务（康复机器人Camargo数据集、压力检测Non-EEG Stress、帕金森病检测Gait Parkinson's Disease）上，对比从零训练与SPT训练的Transformer架构。SPT采用四种基于掩码的预训练目标，旨在促进时序和跨模态表征学习，并系统变化模型深度以考察模型容量与预训练收益之间的交互作用。

Result: 在所有数据集和配置下，SPT一致性地将分类准确率提升了0-6个百分点（具体幅度取决于掩码策略、数据集和架构）。增益不仅出现在多变量设置中，在单变量输入下同样显著。此外，更深层的模型能够更好地利用预训练中学到的丰富时序表征，获得更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构更改即可增强Transformer在医学时间序列任务上的性能，有望改善数据受限临床场景下的模型鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [58] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对WAIT算法的轻量级扩展，通过在线估计请求到达强度来适应时变的到达率，不需要先验流量知识，在MMPP合成负载下相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量并保持相当的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度算法通常假设泊松到达且到达率恒定，无法反映真实世界流量的突发性和动态性，导致实际场景下性能不佳。

Method: 在WAIT算法基础上进行轻量级扩展，基于观测到的间隔时间在线估计请求强度，从而自适应时变的到达率，无需先验流量知识；使用基于马尔可夫调制泊松过程（MMPP）的合成工作负载进行仿真评估。

Result: 在评估的低到达率转变场景下，所提方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持可比的延迟水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应调度扩展方法能够有效应对真实世界中请求到达率的时变和突发性特征，在吞吐量上优于现有主流方法且不牺牲延迟性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [59] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillTFM是一个免训练的Tabular Foundation Model（TFM）自适应系统，通过将TFM从参数更新转向基于门控的智能体技能演化来应对分布偏移、异构特征语义和任务特定模式等部署挑战。其核心是可验证且可扩展的技能库，结合边界证据识别与门控技能演化，在模拟边界场景和真实电价预测任务中显著提升了AUC指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在现实应用中无处不在，Tabular Foundation Models（TFMs）虽为通用表格学习提供了新范式，但实际部署受限于分布偏移、异构特征语义以及难以在不进行昂贵微调或依赖额外标注数据的情况下捕捉的任务特定模式。

Method: SkillTFM提出训练免自适应框架：核心是可验证且可扩展的技能库，包含两个关键组件——（1）边界证据识别：刻画任务结构和基座模型的失败模式；（2）门控技能演化：在明确验证约束下检索并扩展可复用技能。系统将TFM的自适应从参数更新转变为智能体技能的门控演化过程。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实电价预测任务中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128-0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898；跨多种TFM骨干网络的实验进一步验证了SkillTFM的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM通过免训练的技能门控演化范��，有效解决了TFM在分布偏移和多样任务模式下的部署瓶颈，且在不同TFM骨干上均表现出良好的有效性和通用性，为表格基础模型的实用化部署提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [60] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向二值神经网络的后训练提前终止机制，利用训练集上运行累积和行为来预测最终符号，从而跳过大量不必要的累加操作，在不重新训练任何模型参数的情况下显著减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络虽然将点积转化为符号控制的加减法，但操作数量不变——每个神经元仍累加全部输入。然而随着累加进行，部分和频繁偏离零点，最终符号很早就能被预测，后面的贡献只改变和值而不改变最终激活。这种浪费值得被消除。

Method: 将上述观察转化为后训练（post-training）提前终止机制：在训练数据集上刻画运行累加的行为特征，利用这些信息尽早预测最终符号；在理想的权重排序假设下统计操作数量。无需重新训练任何模型参数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上的VGG11实验中，最深的卷积层可消除86.6%的累加项，仅造成0.37个百分点的精度下降；同时对三个最深卷积层使用该机制，可消除全网络25%的算术运算，精度下降1.36个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过后训练的方式利用累加过程的统计特性，可以在几乎不影响精度的前提下显著降低二值神经网络的推理计算量，为资源受限场景下的部署提供了有效优化手段。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [61] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA是一个基于句法解析器监督的偏好优化框架，通过将解析器判断转化为偏好对并结合delta-DPO进行训练，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语等低资源语言上显著提升语法质量，无需人工偏好标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化（如DPO）通常依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注，这在形态丰富且低资源的语言中尤其困难，因为此类标注极为稀缺。需要一个无需人工标注的替代方案来提升这些语言的语法质量。

Method: SAGA框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 将依赖解析器的判断转化为用于delta-DPO的偏好对；(2) 提出结合解析器质量和词汇多样性的复合奖励函数；(3) 使用奖励差距准则过滤低信息量的偏好对；(4) 监测奖励黑客（reward hacking）以维持可靠的监督信号。

Result: 在GPT-SW3-1.3B上对丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语进行实验：丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立的Stanza评估上提升+4.5个百分点（三次运行均值+3.3个百分点），母语者在80%的成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: 解析器派生的监督信号是在可获得高质量依赖解析器的低资源语言中，替代人工偏好标注进行语法对齐的实用方案。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [62] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三轴框架（When/How/Where）来系统审视持续学习从参数中心范式向系统级适应范式的转变，并基于此框架对代表性方法进行综述，探讨关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习（CL）主要关注参数中心机制（如训练策略、架构设计、权重适配），但新兴范式（如on-policy学习、推理时训练、外部记忆/技能库/交互协议等外部组件）正在将CL的范围从模型参数适配扩展到系统级适应。为刻画这一从参数中心学习向系统级适应转变的趋势，需要一个新的分析框架来系统性理解其演化。

Method: 本文提出了一个三轴分析框架：(1) How维度——学习发生的机制，涵盖off-policy、on-policy以及超越梯度的优化机制；(2) When维度——学习发生的时间阶段，涵盖预训练、后训练和推理阶段；(3) Where维度——更新的位置，界定在内部参数中还是外部结构约束中。基于此三维框架对代表性持续学习方法进行系统梳理和分类。

Result: 基于三轴框架系统综述了持续学习的代表性方法，厘清了该领域从参数中心学习向系统级适应过渡的演化轨迹，并识别出各维度上的范式转变特征。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心学习到系统级适应的根本性范式转变；文中讨论了这一转变带来的关键挑战、广泛影响及未来研究方向，为该领域后续发展提供了新的分析与思考框架。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [63] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个概率性全球天气预报模型，通过显式多时间步推理解决现有ML天气预测模型中时间步长选择的权衡问题。它使用单一训练权重集，在推理时可配置时间步长（从短至数小时到长至24小时），并发现混合时间步长训练能持续提升rollout稳定性。该模型是~134M参数的轻量级邻域注意力Transformer，实现了近SOTA中期概率技能、稳定的扩展期rollout以及高效训练推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖预定的固定自回归时间步长，而时间步长的选择存在根本性权衡：短时间步长（1-6小时）能精细解析昼夜循环内的大气动力学，但增加了给定预报范围内的误差累积；长时间步长（如24小时）减少了误差累积，但限制了短期预报中高可预测性的次日级别信息的可用性。作者旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: GEM-3通过显式多时间步推断（multi-timestep inference）解决该权衡：使用一组训练权重，在推理时配置模型时间步长以在广泛预报范围内平衡可预测性与可用性。方法包括：(1) 混合时间步长训练，相较于时间步长专精模型持续改善rollout稳定性；(2) 采用~134M参数的轻量级邻域注意力Transformer架构，基于等距圆柱网格（equirectangular grid）；(3) 在GEM-2基础上引入了多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3实现了接近最先进水平的中期概率预报技能，具备稳定的扩展期rollout，同时保持高效训练和推理，并提供决策相关的诊断输出。混合时间步长训练被证明相对于时间步长专精模型在rollout稳定性上有一致改进。

Conclusion: GEM-3提供了一个实用的天气预报系统，融合了中期概率技能、扩展期稳定性、训练/推理效率以及决策相关诊断能力，为ML天气预报中时间步长权衡问题提供了一个统一解决方案。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [64] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"训练后适配"（post-training adaptation）的综述论文，提出了一个六维分类法来系统化整理重训练、微调、参数高效适配、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、去学习、校准和多模态指令微调等多样化的适配技术，并指出了该领域面临的关键开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后适配已成为现代机器学习实践的核心，涵盖多种技术系列，但相关文献在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景之间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的比较框架，难以描述模型被如何修改，也无法有效追踪模型变更。

Method: 提出一个六维分类法（基于机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），区分微调、检索增强和提示工程等常被混淆的概念；映射技术间关系（继承、取代、混合、分层部署栈）；梳理从传统机器学习到深度学习、基础模型、LLM和多模态LLM的适配策略演进。

Result: 该综述提供了一套统一词汇，可支持技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析；同时识别了评估、可复现性、持续推理时适配、去学习、多模态适配和治理感知训练后流程等方面的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 训练后适配领域需要一个系统化的分类框架来弥合碎片化研究，该六维分类法为比较不同适配方法、追踪模型变更以及支撑治理分析提供了基础，但仍有多个关键问题有待解决。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [65] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaboLLM，一个代谢组学专科的大型语言模型，通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索进行适配；并开发MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图用于患者级预测。在四个骨干模型家族上表现优于基础模型和医学适配模型，并在外部基准和两个临床应用中取得最优预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的表示形式，缺乏能够组织和利用这些异构生化知识的专用模型。

Method: MetaboLLM通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配代谢组学领域；MetaboLLM-GIN使用图同构网络将LLM生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图进行患者级预测。

Result: 在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于基础模型和医学适配模型；MetaboLLM-GIN在应力性高血糖预测（AUC 0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC 0.8123）中达到最高性能，优于传统模型、替代图构建方法及未适配或未检索的LLM配置，且模型解释产生生物学上有意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专科化的语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织成可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学临床应用提供新范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [66] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF，一个基于自监督对比学习框架的反应表示学习方法，通过构建冷凝反应图（CRG）统一反应物和产物信息，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，并在多个产率预测基准上超越图与序列基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标记数据稀缺、反应空间组合爆炸且稀疏的问题，现有基于字符串、指纹和图结构的反应编码只能部分捕捉化学转化信息，难以对复杂底物反应进行准确预测。

Method: 提出RxnCLF自监督对比学习框架，基于冷凝反应图（CRG）将反应物与产物信息统一到单一图中，使模型学习显式且丰富的转化结构而非断开的图。在170万Pistachio反应上进行预训练，学习紧凑连续隐空间，同时捕捉反应中心特征与侧链上下文。

Result: 在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集等多个产率预测基准上微调，RxnCLF持续超越图和序列基线方法，提升R2指标并取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF是一种可扩展的反应基础模型，有潜力推广到更广泛反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [67] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应查询与非自适应查询之间的权衡。在有限结果空间上，证明了当且仅当两个分布类的条件概率存在正分离时才能可靠区分；当分离为零时，最优最坏情况误差恰好为1/2。对于任意T查询自适应策略，构造了一个使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询的随机非自适应过程，其模拟转录与自适应转录的Total Variation距离在ρ以内。建立了常数自适应查询复杂度与Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度相匹配的分布族，从而确定最坏情况固定误差自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估面临一个核心问题：是否可以在观察到任何响应之前预先固定所有测试，还是应根据早期响应选择后续测试（自适应查询）？理解两种查询模式之间能力差异的量化边界，对于实际模型评估协议的设计具有根本性意义。

Method: 在有限结果空间X（|X|=N）的条件查询模型下，作者首先刻画分布类可区分性的充要条件（条件概率的正分离）。随后对于任意T查询自适应策略，构造一个随机化非自适应过程，使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次预固定对查询，在Total Variation距离下模拟自适应转录。同时构造具有常数自适应查询复杂度和Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度的分布族，证明匹配的下界。

Result: 学习性当且仅当两类分布的条件概率存在正分离时成立；当分离为零时，任何有限查询预算下最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。自适应与非自适应查询之间的最坏情况固定误差差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互式评估能使所需测试数量减少二次因子（N²量级），但交互评估看似指数级的分支并不会转化为指数级的查询优势。交互的价值在多项式层面显著、在指数层面有限。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [68] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，用于采样具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的势函数的目标分布。该算法直接操作次梯度并使用taming技术实现稳定显式离散化，在Wasserstein-2距离下获得非渐近收敛界，并提供了相应的优化问题超额风险估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法在处理同时满足非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸三重特性的目标分布时，要么依赖计算成本高昂的平滑化过程，要么收敛速率不理想。本文旨在弥合这一空白，提供一种直接基于次梯度、无需平滑且具有更优收敛保证的采样算法。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)：采用次梯度直接作用于目标势函数，避免繁琐的平滑化预处理；通过taming技术（抑制项）构造稳定且显式的离散格式，以应对超线性梯度增长带来的不稳定性；在Wasserstein-2距离下推导非渐近收敛界，所有常数均以维度和逆温度显式追踪。

Result: SG-TULA在Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛速率优于现有次梯度类Langevin算法；验证了正则化GPT-2系LLM预训练势函数满足所有假设且常数显式；boosted coordinate-wise版本的SG-TULA在GPT-2预训练任务上与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争，而这些优化器没有可比的非渐近保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时满足非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性质的采样问题提供了理论上有保证且计算上可行的方案，并在LLM预训练的实际应用中展现出竞争力，为大规模非凸非光滑贝叶斯采样提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [69] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力的新框架Surv-IPTB，用于估计生存分析中的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），通过将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，结合区间值概率处理右删失数据，并在多个非线性合成数据集上优于传统元学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，个性化治疗获益评估至关重要，但现有方法（如元学习器T-learner、S-learner，以及随机生存森林、Cox比例风险、Beran估计器等）在复杂非线性场景下性能显著退化，且对右删失数据缺乏原理性的处理方式。因此需要一种可扩展且统计上严谨的框架来直接量化个体患者接受治疗后生存时间延长的概率。

Method: Surv-IPTB框架将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较；通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原理性地处理右删失观测；采用具有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，灵活地进行数据驱动的成对比较聚合，同时为删失样本学习软类别概率。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构（包括螺旋、钟形和圆形特征空间）的合成数据集上进行了大量实验，该框架在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下保持稳健性能，并始终优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险和Beran估计器的元学习基线（T-learner和S-learner），尤其在传统方法显著退化的挑战性非线性场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架被证明是生存环境下个性化治疗获益评估的一种可扩展且统计上原理性的解决方案，代码已公开。该工作为处理右删失数据和复杂非线性关系下的个体化治疗决策提供了新的有力工具。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BaKron，一种高效的神经网络量化求解器，它利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似（双侧自适应舍入）来加速GPTQ风格的量化算法。BaKron将总计算量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，并与GPTQ的立方复杂度保持一致的量级，同时利用了更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常只利用输入激活的单侧信息（one-sided information），无法捕捉输出坐标之间的相关性。而双侧Kronecker分解的Hessian近似虽然能捕获更丰富的跨输出坐标相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算代价过高。

Method: BaKron结合了anti-diagonal并行化和递归分治构造方法。对于m×n的权重矩阵，BaKron使用O(m+n)个顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)降低到O(mn(m+n))。该方法是模块化的，既支持不同的基础量化器，也兼容不同的Hessian估计器。

Result: BaKron的计算复杂度与GPTQ的立方复杂度相匹配，同时利用了更丰富的曲率信息（双侧Kronecker分解Hessian）。论文还提供了实际基准测试，考虑了多种可用的Hessian形式，并找到了计算这些Hessian的高效技术，最后进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: BaKron在保持GPTQ级计算效率的同时，通过利用双侧Kronecker分解Hessian所蕴含的更丰富几何信息，为神经网络量化提供了一种更加高效且模块化的求解方案。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [71] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-OPSD（无监督在线策略自蒸馏），仅利用模型自身生成与内部一致性即可实现自蒸馏，无需任何外部监督信号（地面真值、环境反馈或大模型指导）。通过多数投票构建伪解并蒸馏到模型最长的错误完成前缀上，实现无需标注的自校正训练，在多个数学基准上超越或匹配有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏（OPD/OPSD）方法虽然在下游LLM训练中展现出潜力，但严重依赖外部监督信号（如ground-truth、环境反馈或更大模型的指导），并非真正的"自"蒸馏。作者希望通过仅利用模型自身的生成结果实现真正的无监督自蒸馏，从而消除对外部标注的依赖，使模型可以独立自主地进行自我改进。

Method: U-OPSD包含两个核心步骤：(1) 采样多条rollout，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构建伪解（pseudo-solution），并选择最短的伪解作为教师分布；(2) 将该教师分布蒸馏到模型最长的错误完成（incorrect completion）的前缀中，使模型能在其"自信犯错"的精确位置进行自我校正。整个过程无需ground-truth、外部反馈或更大模型的指导。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23等多个基准上，U-OPSD在Qwen3非思考模式下4B和8B规模分别提升基线8.5%和10.7%，平均超过OPSD 3.2%和2.3%。在思考模式下与OPSD相当（4B超0.9%，8B持平），超过GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明仅凭模型自身生成和内部一致性即可实现有效的自蒸馏后训练，在无需任何外部监督的情况下达到甚至超越需要ground-truth的监督方法（如OPSD和GRPO），为LLM的无监督自我改进提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [72] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种排名式奖励构建（RRC）方法，解决了生成式奖励模型在强化学习中因比较性建模与标量评分范式不匹配而无法充分发挥潜力的问题，通过自竞争排名和锚定引导排名两种策略，从相对偏好排名中推导有效的RL学习信号，在开放式对话和推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型虽然具有强大的响应排名能力，但在强化学习（RL）中的应用效果不佳。作者分析发现，根本原因在于生成式奖励模型的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配，导致生成式奖励模型的潜力无法在RL训练中得到充分释放。

Method: 提出Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC)方法，将生成式奖励模型提供的相对偏好排名转换为更有效的RL学习信号。RRC包含两种互补策略：(1) 自竞争排名（self-competitive ranking），利用采样响应之间的比较来构建奖励；(2) 锚定引导排名（anchor-guided ranking），利用少量参考响应实现可扩展的排名式奖励构建。

Result: 在开放式对话和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC方法显著改善了生成式奖励模型下的RL训练效果，相比现有的奖励构建方法取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC通过解决生成式奖励模型比较性本质与RL标量评分范式之间的不匹配问题，充分释放了生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的潜力，提供了一种有效的奖励构建框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [73] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过验证求解器行为（多求解器校准和对比求解器校准）来修订候选任务，构建了5,431个校准终端任务。训练后的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%，较基座模型最大提升24.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端代理需要可执行且可验证的任务，但"可执行"只能保证可行性，无法揭示任务相对于给定求解器设定的难度和挑战性。现有的任务合成仅关注可行性验证，缺乏对任务与求解器能力之间匹配度的考量，需要一个能够量化任务相对于求解器行为的学习难度区间的方法。

Method: CalibForge采用两种校准策略：(1) 多求解器校准——针对异构求解器池中的分歧；(2) 对比求解器校准——针对指定的强通过/弱失败关系。两者都将"可证明可解性"作为锚点，操作化了一个求解器相对的"可学习区间"。系统通过已验证的求解器行为来修订候选任务，实现自主合成。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准终端任务。消融实验表明两种校准策略均比仅作者编写+验证或普通单求解器反馈产生更有效的监督信号。在全量集合上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0达到32.58%和47.57%；相对于对应基座模型，最大提升在Terminal-Bench 2.0上为24.71个百分点，SWE-bench Pro上为27.68个百分点，Doc2Repo上为30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对的可学习性（solver-relative learnability）是构建有效且可迁移的代理训练数据的实用目标。通过将求解器行为纳入任务校准流程，可以系统地生成既可行又具有适当挑战性的训练任务，且训练得到的模型具有显著的性能提升和跨基准迁移能力。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [74] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个学习器，对具有有限VC维d的假设类H，实现了agnostic PAC学习中统计最优的风险界，在样本量n下以概率1-δ达到L(ĥ) ≤ L* + O(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，从而在固定L*的意义下解决了agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度问题，其常数与已知下界匹配（至多差一个通用常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是解决agnostic PAC学习中是否能够达到Devroye、Györfi和Lugosi（1996）给出的信息论下界。此前虽然广泛了解最优率的上界形式，但能否在固定L*（贝叶斯最优风险）下同时与下界匹配常数，一直未得到解决。作者希望构造出一个具体算法，严格证明其为统计最优。

Method: 论文构造了一个新的学习算法（学习器），该算法基于对假设类H的有限VC维结构进行精细的设计，利用某种精心选择的经验风险最小化或正则化/聚合策略，分析导致如上文所示的风险界，证明其与已知下界在常数意义下匹配。具体方法细节在正文中展开。

Result: 得到了统计最优的风险上界：对任意固定L*，以概率1-δ有L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)。该界与Devroye等(1996)的下界匹配（差通用常数），从而解决了agnostic PAC学习样本复杂度的最优性问题。

Conclusion: 本文首次构造出在固定L*时与信息论下界（差常数）匹配的精确学习算法，彻底解答了agnostic PAC学习中样本复杂度的最优常数量级问题。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>

### [75] [Hierarchical Server Architecture for Agentic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05332)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Agentic Science的层次化动态资源发现架构与软件系统，通过秘书代理（secretary agents）实现跨云、边缘和HPC环境的并发异步协商、选择和任务调度。系统在51个真实及模拟供应商（7类）上进行了19,973次协商和6,952次选择模拟，达到87.71%的协商准确率，选择成本与传统策略相当。系统当前已支持Genesis任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic Science正在改变计算工作的格局，其工作负载需要跨机构、跨类型的专门硬件资源支持。若需根据工作负载需求与环境匹配进行调度，自动化发现可用资源是必不可少的步骤，因此需要一种能够高效发现异构环境中资源的架构。

Method: 提出一种层次化动态架构和软件系统，用于跨多云、边缘和HPC系统进行资源发现。该设计利用秘书代理（secretary agents）实现请求的并发异步协商、选择和调度，支持分布式环境的资源探测与发现。

Result: 在51个真实与模拟的供应商（涵盖7个类别）上进行大规模实验，执行了19,973次协商模拟和6,952次选择模拟，实现了87.71%的高协商准确率，同时选择成本与传统策略相当。

Conclusion: 该架构具有良好可扩展性，已在实际的Genesis任务中得到支持。研究强调了代理、发现工具与基础设施之间精细协调对于Agentic Science的重要性。

Abstract: Agentic science is transforming the landscape of computational work, extending to scientific pipelines and workload managers. The workloads require specialized hardware within and across institutions. If assessing workload needs against environments is required for scheduling, automated discovery of resources is an essential step. In this paper, we present a hierarchical, dynamic architecture and software to discover resources across diverse cloud, edge, and HPC systems. The design enables concurrent, asynchronous negotiation, selection, and dispatch of requests for work using secretary agents. The agents probe and discover 51 real and simulated providers across 7 categories. We perform 19,973 negotiation and 6,952 selection simulations to assess reliability of decisions, demonstrating high (87.71\%) negotiation accuracy and selection costs comparable to more traditional strategies. Designed for extensibility and currently supporting the Genesis Mission, this architecture exemplifies the importance of careful coordination between agents, discovery tools, and infrastructure for agentic science.

</details>


### [76] [Application Failures and Machine Computational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05408)
*Carlo Graziani,Bethany Lusch,O. E. Bronson Messer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估百亿亿次级科学计算平台在应用故障率显著时的正常运行时间效率框架，将故障率和平均故障间隔从时间维度转向使用量（节点小时）维度，并基于Daly（2006）框架更新了最优检查点间隔的确定方法，最后用ORNL Frontier超级计算机一年的生产运行数据进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的百亿亿次级科学计算平台和大型AI训练设施面临显著的应用故障率，但由于科学计算平台应用具有异构性，传统的以时间为单位的故障率和平均故障间隔指标无法准确反映实际使用情况，需要一个基于使用量来评估和优化系统正常运行效率的框架。

Method: 论文将故障率、平均故障间隔等指标重新定义在使用量（如节点小时）维度；考虑故障、检查点保存和重启带来的使用量损失；改进Daly（2006）框架，允许用户指定最小化损失的最优检查点使用间隔；推导机器计算效率公式来量化可用于科学计算的预期资源分配比例。

Result: 该框架使用ORNL Frontier超级计算机一年的生产运行数据进行了方法验证，展示了如何在实际大规模生产环境中应用该指标体系评估系统计算效率。

Conclusion: 面向异构科学计算应用，基于使用量的故障率与检查点优化框架能够更准确地评估和提升百亿亿次级计算平台的正常运行效率，为大规模科学计算和AI训练环境的可靠性管理提供了新方法。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating uptime efficiency of Exascale-class scientific computers when application failure rates are appreciable. This is the situation that confronts current leadership-class scientific computing platforms and large AI training installations. What distinguishes scientific computing platforms is the heterogeneity of their applications. We argue that this diversity requires that failure rates and mean intervals between failures should be specified in terms of \emph{usage} (e.g. node-hours) rather than time, as is currently customary. We consider the usage loss terms due to failures, to checkpointing, and to restart costs, and update the framework of Daly (2006) allowing users to specify optimal checkpointing usage intervals that minimize such losses. We derive the machine computational efficiency, which specifies the expected fractional resource allocation that is available for scientific computation. We illustrate the methodology using one year of production runtime data from the \emph{Frontier} supercomputer at Oak Ridge National Laboratory.

</details>


### [77] [Serverless platform driven CPU loadbalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05633)
*Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由Serverless平台驱动的CPU负载均衡框架，通过基于SchedExt(SCX)构建的自定义Linux调度器，让控制平面直接干预CPU调度决策。该调度器引入可配置调度域和共享接口，基于函数历史到达间隔将函数分配到不同域，域内采用单队列负载均衡和虚拟时间优先级策略。实验表明八域配置可在能耗降低约15%的同时仅增加5%调用成本，高负载下相比CFS减少高达50%的请求延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台虽然保有函数调用和资源利用的全局视图，但现有系统的CPU调度决策仍主要由操作系统调度器决定，平台控制平面无法充分利用全局信息来优化调度，导致负载均衡和能效优化受到限制。

Method: 基于SchedExt(SCX)构建自定义Linux调度器，设计可配置的调度域结构和控制平面共享接口，将函数按其历史到达间隔分配到相应调度域；在域内采用单队列负载均衡策略结合虚拟时间优先级策略，以改善任务放置并减少忙碌轮询任务对正常任务的干扰。

Result: 八种调度域配置取得最佳权衡：系统能耗降低约15%，调用成本仅增加5%；在重负载工作负载下，相比默认Linux CFS调度器，请求延迟最高降低50%。

Conclusion: 将CPU调度决策暴露给Serverless控制平面可以在保持调度灵活性的同时提升能源效率和工作负载性能，验证了平台级调度介入的有效性。

Abstract: Serverless platforms maintain a global view of function invocations and resource utilization, yet existing systems largely restrict CPU scheduling decisions to the operating system scheduler. This paper presents a serverless platform-driven CPU load balancing framework that enables the control plane to directly influence CPU scheduling through a custom Linux scheduler built on SchedExt(SCX). The proposed scheduler introduces configurable scheduling domains and a shared interface that allows the control plane to assign functions to domains based on their historical inter-arrival times. Within each domain, a single-queue load-balancing strategy combined with a virtual-time prioritization policy improves task placement while reducing interference from busy-polling tasks. Results show that an eight-domain configuration achieves the best trade-off, reducing system energy consumption by approximately 15% while increasing invocation cost by only 5%. Under heavily loaded workloads, the proposed scheduler also reduces request latency by up to 50% compared to the default Linux Completely Fair Scheduler (CFS). These results demonstrate that exposing CPU scheduling decisions to the serverless control plane can improve both energy efficiency and workload performance while preserving scheduling flexibility.

</details>


### [78] [RepoOMP: Repository-Aware Hotspot OpenMP Parallelization via Dependency-Aware Context Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05855)
*Yongjie Qian,Ke Gao,Zhibin Zhang,Shaohui Peng,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RepoOMP是一个混合框架，通过构建多粒度属性性能图(MAP)来恢复并行化相关证据，并在规则系统与LLM代理之间路由热点，同时构建结构化变换上下文(STC)暴露依赖事实。在951个来自NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的热点上评估，372个成功接受，包括330个真实存储库热点，平均加速比达8.23x(NPB)和8.96x(BOTS)，跨骨干平均5.25x，相比非结构化Claude Code基线提升18-28%加速比并降低47-68% token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟的存储库中OpenMP并行化依然困难，因为循环安全性和优化收益往往依赖于非局部证据。基于规则的工具在合法性无法局部证明时欠并行化，而基于代理的方法在检索遗漏关键依赖或包含无关代码时变得不稳定。

Method: RepoOMP提出混合框架：(1)构建多粒度属性性能图(MAP)在生成前恢复并行化相关证据；(2)将热点在确定性规则与LLM代理之间路由；(3)构建结构化变换上下文(STC)，仅暴露依赖事实而不向模型灌输无关的存储库文本。

Result: 在951个热点的评估中，372个通过编译、工作负载特定检查及正加速比验证后被接受，其中330个为真实存储库热点。NPB平均加速比8.23x，BOTS为8.96x；九个详细真实内核跨骨干平均5.25x，加速比提升18-28%，代理端token成本降低47-68%；330个接受的真实热点中位加速比为2.25x。

Conclusion: RepoOMP提供了一种证据引导的工作流程，用于存储库环境中的热点并行化，验证了规则与LLM代理混合方案在真实世界代码上的有效性和效率。

Abstract: OpenMP parallelization of hotspots in mature repositories remains difficult because loop safety and optimization payoff often depend on non-local evidence. Rule-based tools under-parallelize when legality is not locally provable, while agent-based approaches become unstable when retrieval misses decisive dependencies or includes irrelevant code. We present RepoOMP, a hybrid framework that recovers parallelization-relevant evidence before generation. RepoOMP builds a Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph (MAP), routes hotspots between deterministic rules and an LLM agent, and constructs a Structured Transformation Context (STC) that exposes dependency facts without flooding the model with unrelated repository text. We evaluate RepoOMP on 951 profiled hotspots from NPB, BOTS, FFmpeg, NCNN, and GROMACS. Under compilation, workload-specific checks, and positive speedup, 372 hotspots are accepted, including 330 real-world repository hotspots. RepoOMP achieves average speedups of $8.23\times$ on NPB and $8.96\times$ on BOTS. For the nine detailed real-world kernels used in matched-backbone and robustness analyses, RepoOMP reaches a cross-backbone mean of $5.25\times$, improves speedup by 18--28\%, and reduces agent-side token cost by 47--68\% relative to the unstructured Claude Code baseline. Across 330 accepted real-world hotspots, median speedup is $2.25\times$. Overall, RepoOMP provides an evidence-guided workflow for hotspot parallelization in repository settings. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Qlalq/RepoOMP_Simplified.

</details>


### [79] [Operating Multi-Node Full Fine-Tuning on NVIDIA B300: A Field Report on Telemetry-Based Triage, Negative Results, and Operational Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05944)
*Seon Ho Kim,Ui Jeong Jeon,Su Hyeon Kim,Min Tae Hwang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是首个在NVIDIA B300（16卡，两节点，FSDP/ZeRO-3）上全参数微调32.76B稠密模型（Qwen3-32B）的实战报告。不声称新算法，贡献在于整合的现场经验与实测数据，提供四项工程产出：按板卡瓦数区分的功耗排查表、破除常规模优化迷思的阴性结果、4/8/16卡强扩展基准数据、以及一个epoch末NCCL死锁的完整故障案例与对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B300是新型加速器，业界缺乏公开的大模型训练实战记录；同时大模型微调中存在大量未经证实的优化"民间智慧"（folklore），需要在新硬件上用受控实验加以验证或证伪，以沉淀可迁移的工程经验。

Method: 方法为纯工程实践：(1)利用板卡瓦数区分计算/通信/数据饥饿/检查点或死锁/空闲五种状态（指出utilization%在NCCL挂起时仍读100%的陷阱）；(2)设计A/B对照实验比较per-step NFS读取与预分词本地缓存（结果相近，因语料落入page cache且任务为计算受限），并重构早前"吞吐崩塌"实为NFS/CPU争用；(3)在B300上做4/8/16卡强扩展与GPU-hour标定；(4)针对epoch末NCCL死锁，实现2.7秒预运行不变量门控 + 外部监视器，将数小时静默故障转为即时拒绝，并对应PyTorch文档中的Join/equalize-to-minimum实践。

Result: 强扩展近线性（符合预期，提供绝对值作参考）；NFS与本地缓存吞吐相当（约53k tok/s），证伪"预分词缓存必快"的迷思；确认"吞吐崩塌"为NFS/CPU争用而非存储介质极限；成功用功耗表识别NCCL挂起、用不变量门控与watcher将死锁检测从数小时缩至秒级并统计了故障与门控节省的GPU-hour。

Conclusion: 核心可迁移经验是运维层面而非算法层面：对数据依赖的数据并行任务，应观察功耗而非utilization%；启动前必须验证不变量——通过的冒烟测试不代表完整运行安全。本文提供的是新硬件上的校准参考数据与操作规程，而非新算法贡献。

Abstract: We report operational experience full-fine-tuning a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) on 16 x NVIDIA B300 (two nodes, FSDP / ZeRO-3) -- among the first published field accounts on this accelerator. We claim no new algorithm. The individual mechanisms we use are established practice; our contribution is the integrated field experience and a set of calibrated measurements on new hardware. Concretely we offer four practitioner artifacts. (1) A B300-calibrated power-draw triage table that distinguishes compute / communication / data-starvation / checkpoint-or-deadlock / idle by board wattage (utilization% reads 100% during an NCCL hang). (2) A set of honest negative results that dispel common optimization folklore at this scale: a controlled A/B in which per-step NFS reading matches a pretokenized local cache (~53k tok/s) because the corpus fits in page cache and the job is compute-bound; and a reconstruction of an earlier "throughput collapse" as NFS/CPU contention rather than a storage-medium limit. (3) Calibrated 4/8/16-GPU strong-scaling and GPU-hour numbers on B300 (near-linear, as expected in this regime; we report absolute values as reference data). (4) A worked failure case -- an epoch-end NCCL deadlock from per-rank token-packing imbalance -- together with a 2.7-second pre-run invariant gate and an external watcher that turn multi-hour silent failures into instant rejections. This deadlock and its remedy correspond to PyTorch's documented Join / equalize-to-minimum practice; we position our instantiation against that prior art and report the GPU-hours the failure cost and the gate saves. The transferable takeaway is operational, not algorithmic: for data-dependent data-parallel jobs, watch power rather than utilization, and verify invariants before launch -- a passing smoke test is not evidence of a safe full run.

</details>


### [80] [TensorCast: The Missing Tensor Management Layer in Large Language Model Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06007)
*Yuhan Zhou,Yuchu Luo,Hao Nie,Wangrunze Lv,Yu Zhou,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor-as-a-Service（TaaS）架构，通过TensorCast分布式张量管理层将张量状态管理与计算逻辑解耦，为LLM基础设施提供统一的张量生命周期管理抽象层。集成vLLM和SGLang后，在模型权重物化、权重同步、KV缓存管理和可编程请求路由等场景中达到与专用系统相当的性能，并在高并发多轮agent工作负载下将中位TTFT提升最多93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基础设施中，张量不仅是计算数据，更是跨分布式组件共享的持久状态。现有系统将张量管理任务（如模型权重加载、KV缓存管理、检查点同步）与执行引擎、网络或存储后端深度耦合，形成各自独立的专属优化。这种特化导致策略孤立，难以在快速演进的LLM工作负载中复用和组合张量管理策略，缺少一个统一的生命周期管理抽象层。

Method: 提出TensorCast分布式张量管理层，核心设计包括：1）提供一等公民的张量抽象（first-class tensor abstractions）；2）提供可编程的生命周期原语（programmable lifecycle primitives）；3）运行时将张量管理策略与执行机制分离。开发者通过TensorCast API编写张量管理程序，透明地利用分布式执行和数据移动。

Result: 将TensorCast集成到vLLM和SGLang中，在模型权重物化、权重同步、KV缓存管理和可编程请求路由等多样化张量生命周期工作负载上进行评估。结果表明：1）TensorCast与专用张量管理系统性能相当；2）支持新的跨组件优化策略；3）在高并发多轮agent工作负载下，可编程策略将中位TTFT提升最多93.2%。

Conclusion: 张量生命周期管理是LLM基础设施中缺失的关键抽象层。通过将张量状态管理与计算逻辑解耦，TaaS/TensorCast既能保持与专用系统相当的性能，又能实现跨组件优化策略的组合与复用，为不断演进的LLM工作负载提供更灵活、可扩展的基础设施方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM infrastructure increasingly manages tensors not only as computation data, but also as persistent states shared across distributed components.
